namespace = gr_obituary

# gr_obituary.1 removed in v15.4.3 - on_action now triggers gr_obituary.10 directly

# ================================================
# FULL REMEMBRANCE - The Chronicle
# ================================================
gr_obituary.10 = {
	type = character_event
	title = gr_remembrance_title
	
	immediate = {
		# AGOT SUBMOD: Override chronicler_type to flag:maester if chronicler has maester trait
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = gr_chronicler
				var:gr_chronicler = { has_trait = maester }
			}
			set_variable = { name = gr_chronicler_type value = flag:maester }
		}
		
		# Save heir (ROOT) as explicit scope for localization
		save_scope_as = heir_scope
		
		# Store heir as variable for localization (proven Var().Char pattern)
		set_variable = { name = gr_heir_char value = root }
		
		# Store faith for deity button localization
		set_variable = { name = gr_heir_faith value = root.faith }
		
		# Realm consolidation detection: heir ended up at higher tier
		# than dead ruler (partition created a title on succession)
		set_variable = { name = gr_heir_tier value = root.primary_title.tier }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = gr_tier
				NOT = { has_variable = gr_realm_split }
				var:gr_heir_tier > var:gr_tier
			}
			set_variable = { name = gr_realm_consolidated value = yes }
			set_variable = { name = gr_consolidated_title value = root.primary_title }
		}
		remove_variable = gr_heir_tier
		
		# Save scopes for portrait rendering
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gr_predecessor }
			var:gr_predecessor = { save_scope_as = portrait_predecessor }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gr_chronicler }
			var:gr_chronicler = { save_scope_as = portrait_chronicler }
		}
		
		# Random seed for text-variant selection -- one uniform roll (audit M12).
		#
		# Only rolled if absent. Base gr_on_actions.txt Section A rolls it during
		# on_death, before Section Q copies scope:heir's vars onto the chronicle
		# page artifact. Re-rolling here would overwrite the value the page
		# recorded, and replay would show different text than the death popup.
		# The fallback roll covers saves where a death predates that fix.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = gr_random_seed } }
			random_list = {
				1 = { set_variable = { name = gr_random_seed value = 0 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = gr_random_seed value = 20 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = gr_random_seed value = 40 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = gr_random_seed value = 60 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = gr_random_seed value = 80 } }
				1 = { set_variable = { name = gr_random_seed value = 100 } }
			}
		}
		# Safety defaults for variables that may not be set
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = gr_dread } } set_variable = { name = gr_dread value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = gr_gold } } set_variable = { name = gr_gold value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = gr_tier } } set_variable = { name = gr_tier value = 0 } }
		# ================================================
		# AGOT SUBMOD: Detect dead char's traits and copy war vars onto heir (ROOT)
		# Only runs for real deaths (var:gr_predecessor != root).
		# Debug tests set AGOT vars directly on root before triggering gr_obituary.10.
		# ================================================
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = gr_predecessor
				NOT = { var:gr_predecessor ?= root }
			}
			# --- Dragon traits ---
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = dragonrider } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_dragonrider value = yes } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = dragonless_dragonrider } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_dragonless value = yes } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = dragonwidowed } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_dragonwidowed value = yes } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = dragonslayer } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_dragonslayer value = yes } }
			# --- Sworn service ---
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = kingsguard } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_kingsguard value = yes } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = nightswatch } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_nightswatch value = yes }
				if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = lifestyle_nw_ranger } }
					set_variable = { name = gr_agot_nw_lifestyle value = flag:ranger } }
				else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = lifestyle_nw_builder } }
					set_variable = { name = gr_agot_nw_lifestyle value = flag:builder } }
				else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = lifestyle_nw_steward } }
					set_variable = { name = gr_agot_nw_lifestyle value = flag:steward } }
			}
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = deserter } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_deserter value = yes } }
			# --- Knighthood ---
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = knight } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_knight value = yes } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = squire } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_squire value = yes } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = stripped_knight } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_stripped_knight value = yes } }
			# --- Citadel ---
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = archmaester } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_maester value = flag:archmaester } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = disgraced_maester } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_maester value = flag:disgraced } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = maester } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_maester value = flag:maester } }
			# --- Religious roles ---
			# high_septon trait removed upstream; most_devout_member now covers the top tier
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = most_devout_member } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_religious_role value = flag:most_devout } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = septon } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_religious_role value = flag:septon } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = red_priest } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_religious_role value = flag:red_priest } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = drowned_man } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_religious_role value = flag:drowned_man } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = bearded_priest } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_religious_role value = flag:bearded_priest } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = silent_sister } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_religious_role value = flag:silent_sister } }
			# --- Conditions ---
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = greyscale } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_greyscale value = yes } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = burned } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_burned value = yes } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = one_handed } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_one_handed value = yes } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = crippled } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_crippled value = yes } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = mute } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_mute value = yes } }
			# --- Mystical ---
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = dragon_dreams } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_dragon_dreams value = yes } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = greensight } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_greensight value = yes } }
			# --- Personality ---
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = authoritative } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_authoritative value = yes } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = rude } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_rude value = yes } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = inquisitive } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_inquisitive value = yes } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = uncouth } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_uncouth value = yes } }
			# --- Social / reputation ---
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = paramour } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_paramour value = yes } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = spider } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_spider value = yes } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = accidental_kinslayer } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_kinslayer_accidental value = yes } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = broke_vows } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_broke_vows value = yes } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = fire_obsessed } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_fire_obsessed value = yes } }
			# --- Faith detection ---
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_seven_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:seven } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_pact_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:old_gods } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_ways_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:drowned } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_rhllor_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:rhllor } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_flames_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:valyrian_faith } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_counsels_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:dothraki_faith } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_graces_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:harpy } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_cults_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:free_cities_faith } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_mother_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:rhoynish_faith } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_others_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:cold_gods } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_courts_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:yiti_faith } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_liturgies_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:sarnori_faith } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_orders_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:essos_orders } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_songs_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:moon_singers } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_temples_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:summer_faith } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_houses_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:qartheen_faith } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_flocks_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:shepherd_faith } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_shanties_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:basilisk_faith } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_chosen_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:naathi_faith } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_first_gods_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:ancient_faith } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_venerations_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:ibbenese_faith } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { faith = { religion = religion:the_churches_religion } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_faith value = flag:eldritch_faith } }
			# --- Heritage detection ---
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_andal } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:andal } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_first_man } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:first_men } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_ironman } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:ironborn } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_rhoynar } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:rhoynar } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_freecities } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:free_cities_cult } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_valyrian } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:valyrian_cult } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_grasslands } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:grasslands } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_ghiscari } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:ghiscari } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_yitish } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:yitish } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_jadeislands } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:jade_islands } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_wildling } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:wildling } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_sarnori } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:sarnori_cult } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_summer } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:summer_cult } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_hairyman } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:ibbenese_cult } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_shadowlanders } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:shadowlander } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_elder_races } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:elder_races } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_hyrkoonan } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:hyrkoonan } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_moraqi } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:moraqi } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_nghai } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:nghai } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_qaathi } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:qaathi } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_sothoryi } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:sothoryi } }
			else_if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_mennight } } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_heritage value = flag:mennight } }
			# --- War vars (copy from dead char to heir) ---
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_variable = gr_agot_war_reyne } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_war_reyne value = var:gr_predecessor.var:gr_agot_war_reyne } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_variable = gr_agot_war_blackfyre } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_war_blackfyre value = var:gr_predecessor.var:gr_agot_war_blackfyre } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_variable = gr_agot_war_ninepenny } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_war_ninepenny value = var:gr_predecessor.var:gr_agot_war_ninepenny } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_variable = gr_agot_war_faegon } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_war_faegon value = var:gr_predecessor.var:gr_agot_war_faegon } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_variable = gr_agot_war_daemon } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_war_daemon value = var:gr_predecessor.var:gr_agot_war_daemon } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_variable = gr_agot_war_duskendale } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_war_duskendale value = var:gr_predecessor.var:gr_agot_war_duskendale } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_variable = gr_agot_war_dragon } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_war_dragon value = var:gr_predecessor.var:gr_agot_war_dragon } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_variable = gr_agot_war_dragon_tier } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_war_dragon_tier value = var:gr_predecessor.var:gr_agot_war_dragon_tier } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_variable = gr_agot_war_ranging } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_war_ranging value = var:gr_predecessor.var:gr_agot_war_ranging } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_variable = gr_agot_war_wall_reclaim } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_war_wall_reclaim value = var:gr_predecessor.var:gr_agot_war_wall_reclaim } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_variable = gr_agot_war_rebellion } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_war_rebellion value = var:gr_predecessor.var:gr_agot_war_rebellion } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_variable = gr_agot_war_succession } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_war_succession value = var:gr_predecessor.var:gr_agot_war_succession } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_variable = gr_agot_war_independence } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_war_independence value = var:gr_predecessor.var:gr_agot_war_independence } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_variable = gr_agot_war_pirate } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_war_pirate value = var:gr_predecessor.var:gr_agot_war_pirate } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_variable = gr_agot_war_slavery } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_war_slavery value = var:gr_predecessor.var:gr_agot_war_slavery } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_variable = gr_agot_war_eradicate } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_war_eradicate value = var:gr_predecessor.var:gr_agot_war_eradicate } }
			# --- Government type (AGOT-aware flags from on_government_change hook) ---
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_variable = gr_agot_govt_old } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_govt_old value = var:gr_predecessor.var:gr_agot_govt_old }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_govt_new value = var:gr_predecessor.var:gr_agot_govt_new } }
			# --- Additional physical conditions ---
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = half_blind } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_half_blind value = yes } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = the_mark } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_the_mark value = yes } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = shivers } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_shivers value = yes } }
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_trait = scurvy } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_scurvy value = yes } }
			# --- Iron Bank / Banking ---
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_character_flag = final_default } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_bank_defaulted value = yes } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					var:gr_predecessor = {
						has_variable = IB_Shares
						var:IB_Shares > 0
					}
				}
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_bank_ib_shareholder value = yes }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						var:gr_predecessor = { has_variable = bank1_Shares var:bank1_Shares > 0 }
						var:gr_predecessor = { has_variable = bank2_Shares var:bank2_Shares > 0 }
						var:gr_predecessor = { has_variable = bank3_Shares var:bank3_Shares > 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_bank_founder value = yes }
			}
			if = { limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_character_flag = has_a_loan_flag } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_bank_died_in_debt value = yes } }
			# --- Iron Throne: special opening ---
			if = {
				limit = { var:gr_predecessor = { has_title = title:h_the_iron_throne } }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_iron_throne value = yes }
				set_variable = { name = gr_agot_sub_kingdom_count value = 0 }
				var:gr_predecessor = {
					every_held_title = {
						limit = {
							tier = kingdom
							NOT = { this = title:h_the_iron_throne }
						}
						root = { change_variable = { name = gr_agot_sub_kingdom_count add = 1 } }
					}
				}
			}
		}

		# watermark effect; the DEATH POPUP renders the same replaced loc and
		# must convert too, or every year in it renders blank.
		gr_submod_book_years_effect = yes

			desc = gr_augustus_neutral
		}
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				has_variable = gr_fame_augustus
				var:gr_opinion < -24
			}
			desc = gr_augustus_neg
		}
		
		# ================================================
		# 4b. FOUNDATIONAL ACTS (Government, Faith, Culture, Capital)
		# These are the realm-defining decisions of a reign.
		# ================================================
		
		# --- GOVERNMENT CHANGE ---
		# Tribal to Feudal (positive)
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				has_variable = gr_memory_govt_change
				var:gr_govt_old = flag:tribal
				var:gr_govt_new = flag:feudal
				var:gr_opinion >= 0
			}
			desc = gr_govt_tribal_to_feudal_pos
		}
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				has_variable = gr_memory_govt_change
				var:gr_govt_old = flag:tribal
				var:gr_govt_new = flag:feudal
				var:gr_opinion < 0
			}
			desc = gr_govt_tribal_to_feudal_neg
		}
		# Tribal to Clan
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				has_variable = gr_memory_govt_change
				var:gr_govt_old = flag:tribal
				var:gr_govt_new = flag:clan
				var:gr_opinion >= 0
			}
			desc = gr_govt_tribal_to_clan_pos
		}
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				has_variable = gr_memory_govt_change
				var:gr_govt_old = flag:tribal
				var:gr_govt_new = flag:clan
				var:gr_opinion < 0
			}
			desc = gr_govt_tribal_to_clan_neg
		}
		# Feudal to Administrative
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				has_variable = gr_memory_govt_change
				var:gr_govt_old = flag:feudal
				var:gr_govt_new = flag:admin
				var:gr_opinion >= 0
			}
			desc = gr_govt_feudal_to_admin_pos

				has_variable = gr_memory_govt_change

				var:gr_govt_new = flag:admin
				var:gr_opinion < 0

		}
		# Clan to Feudal
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				has_variable = gr_memory_govt_change

		}

				has_variable = gr_memory_govt_change

				var:gr_opinion < 0
			}
			desc = gr_govt_nomad_settled_neg
		}
		# Generic fallback (any other government change)
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				has_variable = gr_memory_govt_change
				NOR = {
					AND = { var:gr_govt_old = flag:tribal var:gr_govt_new = flag:feudal }
					AND = { var:gr_govt_old = flag:tribal var:gr_govt_new = flag:clan }
					AND = { var:gr_govt_old = flag:feudal var:gr_govt_new = flag:admin }
					AND = { var:gr_govt_old = flag:clan var:gr_govt_new = flag:feudal }
					AND = { var:gr_govt_old = flag:clan var:gr_govt_new = flag:admin }
					var:gr_govt_old = flag:nomad
				}
				NOT = { has_variable = gr_agot_govt_old }
				var:gr_opinion >= 0
			}
			desc = gr_govt_generic_pos
		}
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				has_variable = gr_memory_govt_change
				NOR = {
					AND = { var:gr_govt_old = flag:tribal var:gr_govt_new = flag:feudal }
					AND = { var:gr_govt_old = flag:tribal var:gr_govt_new = flag:clan }
					AND = { var:gr_govt_old = flag:feudal var:gr_govt_new = flag:admin }
					AND = { var:gr_govt_old = flag:clan var:gr_govt_new = flag:feudal }
					AND = { var:gr_govt_old = flag:clan var:gr_govt_new = flag:admin }
					var:gr_govt_old = flag:nomad
				}
				NOT = { has_variable = gr_agot_govt_old }
				var:gr_opinion < 0
			}
			desc = gr_govt_generic_neg
		}
		# AGOT: Lord Paramount transitions
		triggered_desc = {

				has_variable = gr_agot_govt_old

		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				OR = {
					has_variable = gr_fame_vetadventurer
					has_variable = gr_memory_varangian
					has_variable = gr_memory_adventure
					has_variable = gr_memory_created_duchy
					has_variable = gr_memory_created_kingdom
					has_variable = gr_memory_created_empire
					has_variable = gr_memory_claimed_hegemony

					has_variable = gr_memory_usurped_hegemony
					has_variable = gr_memory_conquered_realm
					has_variable = gr_fame_papacy_destroyed
					has_variable = gr_fame_augustus
					has_variable = gr_memory_govt_change
					has_variable = gr_memory_faith_created
					has_variable = gr_memory_faith_convert
					has_variable = gr_memory_culture_created
					has_variable = gr_memory_capital_moved

		}

		# ================================================
		# 6. CHARACTER PORTRAIT - The Five Sentences
		# ================================================
		
		# ---- SENTENCE 1: IDENTITY (Education + Fame + Top Stat) ----
		# Compound entries first (education + fame combos), then fallbacks
		
		# --- Nomadic philosophy (active nomad vs settled) ---
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					has_variable = gr_fame_nomad
					has_variable = gr_fame_nomad_active

				}

			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					has_variable = gr_fame_nomad
					NOT = { has_variable = gr_fame_nomad_active }
					var:gr_opinion >= 0
				}
				desc = gr_s1_nomad_settled_pos
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					has_variable = gr_fame_nomad
					NOT = { has_variable = gr_fame_nomad_active }
					var:gr_opinion < 0
				}
				desc = gr_s1_nomad_settled_neg
			}
			# --- Confucian education (overrides normal education) ---
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					has_variable = gr_fame_confucian
					has_variable = gr_edu
					var:gr_edu_tier >= 4
				}
				desc = gr_s1_confucian_brilliant
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					has_variable = gr_fame_confucian
					has_variable = gr_edu
					var:gr_edu_tier >= 2
				}
				desc = gr_s1_confucian_competent
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_variable = gr_fame_confucian }
				desc = gr_s1_confucian_basic
			}
			# --- Governor (overrides normal education) ---
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					has_variable = gr_fame_governor
					has_variable = gr_edu
					var:gr_edu_tier >= 4
				}
				desc = gr_s1_governor_brilliant
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					has_variable = gr_fame_governor
					has_variable = gr_edu
					var:gr_edu_tier >= 2
				}
				desc = gr_s1_governor_competent
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { has_variable = gr_fame_governor }
				desc = gr_s1_governor_basic
			}
			# --- Martial education + Berserker/Viking/Adventurer ---
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = {
					has_variable = gr_edu
					var:gr_edu = flag:martial

		# 8. TREASURY
		# ================================================
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = { var:gr_gold >= 500 var:gr_opinion >= 0 }
			desc = gr_treasury_rich
		}
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = { var:gr_gold >= 500 var:gr_opinion < 0 }
			desc = gr_treasury_rich_neg
		}
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				var:gr_gold >= 100
				var:gr_gold < 500
				var:gr_opinion >= 0
			}
			desc = gr_treasury_modest
		}
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = {
				var:gr_gold >= 100
				var:gr_gold < 500
				var:gr_opinion < 0
			}
			desc = gr_treasury_modest_neg
		}
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = { var:gr_gold < 100 var:gr_opinion >= 0 }
			desc = gr_treasury_poor
		}
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = { var:gr_gold < 100 var:gr_opinion < 0 }
			desc = gr_treasury_poor_neg
		}

		}
		
		# ================================================
		# 10b. SCORE NARRATIVE (top category + second category + breakdown)
		# ================================================
		
		# Top category narrative
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { var:gr_score_top_cat = flag:foundation var:gr_opinion >= 0 }
				desc = gr_score_cat_foundation_pos
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { var:gr_score_top_cat = flag:foundation var:gr_opinion < 0 }
				desc = gr_score_cat_foundation_neg
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { var:gr_score_top_cat = flag:warfare var:gr_opinion >= 0 }
				desc = gr_score_cat_warfare_pos
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { var:gr_score_top_cat = flag:warfare var:gr_opinion < 0 }
				desc = gr_score_cat_warfare_neg
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { var:gr_score_top_cat = flag:stewardship var:gr_opinion >= 0 }
				desc = gr_score_cat_stewardship_pos
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { var:gr_score_top_cat = flag:stewardship var:gr_opinion < 0 }
				desc = gr_score_cat_stewardship_neg
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { var:gr_score_top_cat = flag:diplomacy var:gr_opinion >= 0 }
				desc = gr_score_cat_diplomacy_pos
			}
			triggered_desc = {

		first_valid = {

				desc = gr_score_second_foundation
			}

				trigger = { var:gr_score_second_cat = flag:warfare var:gr_score_second_val >= 50 }

		# ================================================
		# 10c. CLOSING WITH SIGNATURE
		# ================================================
		
		# Numeric breakdown (end of chronicle, before motto and signature)
		desc = gr_score_breakdown
		
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = { has_variable = gr_predecessor }
			desc = gr_agot_house_motto_close
		}
		
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { var:gr_chronicler_type = flag:maester }
				desc = gr_signature_maester
			}

	theme = death
	
	left_portrait = {
		trigger = { exists = scope:portrait_predecessor }
		character = scope:portrait_predecessor
		animation = death
	}
	
	right_portrait = {
		trigger = { exists = scope:portrait_chronicler }
		character = scope:portrait_chronicler
		animation = writing
	}
	
	orphan = no
	
	# ================================================
	# OPTION 1: Proclaim the Dynasty (Always Available)
	# Grants renown reward + dynasty prestige modifier
	# ================================================
	option = {
		name = gr_option_proclaim
		
		dynasty = {
			add_dynasty_prestige = root.var:gr_renown_reward
		}
		
		# Dynasty prestige modifier for 15 years
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gr_dynasty_legacy_proclaimed
			years = 15
		}
		
		# ================================================
		# CHRONICLE: Create or upgrade dynasty chronicle
		# Only happens on Legendary score with a chronicler
		# ================================================
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:gr_score_tier = flag:legendary
				has_variable = gr_chronicler
				exists = dynasty
			}
			
			# UPGRADE existing chronicle
			if = {
				limit = { dynasty = { has_variable = gr_has_chronicle } }
				
				dynasty = {
					change_variable = { name = gr_chronicles_recorded add = 1 }
				}
				
				# Find and destroy old chronicle artifact
				random_character_artifact = {
					limit = { has_variable = gr_is_chronicle }
					save_scope_as = old_chronicle
				}
				if = {
					limit = { exists = scope:old_chronicle }
					destroy_artifact = scope:old_chronicle
				}
				
				# Create upgraded artifact based on chronicle count
				# ILLUSTRIOUS (10+ chronicles)
				if = {
					limit = { dynasty = { var:gr_chronicles_recorded >= 10 } }
					dynasty = { set_variable = { name = gr_chronicle_tier value = 4 } }
					create_artifact = {
						name = gr_chronicle_artifact_name
						creator = var:gr_chronicler
						description = gr_chronicle_artifact_desc_illustrious
						type = book
						visuals = book
						quality = 100
						wealth = 80
						max_durability = 100
						modifier = gr_chronicle_illustrious
						save_scope_as = new_chronicle
					}
				}
				# FAMED (6+ chronicles)
				else_if = {
					limit = { dynasty = { var:gr_chronicles_recorded >= 6 } }
					dynasty = { set_variable = { name = gr_chronicle_tier value = 3 } }
					create_artifact = {
						name = gr_chronicle_artifact_name
						creator = var:gr_chronicler
						description = gr_chronicle_artifact_desc_famed
						type = book
						visuals = book
						quality = 80
						wealth = 50
						max_durability = 100
						modifier = gr_chronicle_famed
						save_scope_as = new_chronicle
					}
				}
				# MASTERWORK (3+ chronicles)
				else_if = {
					limit = { dynasty = { var:gr_chronicles_recorded >= 3 } }
					dynasty = { set_variable = { name = gr_chronicle_tier value = 2 } }
					create_artifact = {
						name = gr_chronicle_artifact_name
						creator = var:gr_chronicler
						description = gr_chronicle_artifact_desc_masterwork
						type = book
						visuals = book
						quality = 40
						wealth = 20
						max_durability = 100
						modifier = gr_chronicle_masterwork
						save_scope_as = new_chronicle
					}
				}
				# STILL COMMON (under 3 chronicles)
				else = {
					create_artifact = {
						name = gr_chronicle_artifact_name
						creator = var:gr_chronicler
						description = gr_chronicle_artifact_desc_common
						type = book
						visuals = book
						quality = 10
						max_durability = 100
						modifier = gr_chronicle_common
						save_scope_as = new_chronicle
					}
				}
				
				# Tag new artifact
				if = {
					limit = { exists = scope:new_chronicle }
					scope:new_chronicle = {
						set_variable = { name = gr_is_chronicle value = yes }
						set_variable = { name = gr_chronicle_dynasty value = root.house }
					}
				}
			}
			# CREATE new chronicle
			else = {
				create_artifact = {
					name = gr_chronicle_artifact_name
					creator = var:gr_chronicler
					description = gr_chronicle_artifact_desc_common
					type = book
					visuals = book
					quality = 10
					max_durability = 100
					modifier = gr_chronicle_common
					save_scope_as = new_chronicle
				}
				
				scope:new_chronicle = {
					set_variable = { name = gr_is_chronicle value = yes }
					set_variable = { name = gr_chronicle_dynasty value = root.house }
				}
				
				dynasty = {
					set_variable = { name = gr_has_chronicle value = yes }
					set_variable = { name = gr_chronicles_recorded value = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = gr_chronicle_tier value = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
		
		# ================================================
		# TOOLTIP: Chronicle progress information
		# ================================================
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:gr_score_tier = flag:legendary
				has_variable = gr_chronicler
				exists = dynasty
			}
			custom_tooltip = gr_tooltip_chronicle_recorded
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { var:gr_score_tier = flag:legendary }
			}
			custom_tooltip = gr_tooltip_chronicle_need_legendary
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_variable = gr_chronicler }
			}
			custom_tooltip = gr_tooltip_chronicle_need_chronicler
		}
	}
	
	# ================================================
	# OPTION 3: "My Time Has Come" (Ambitious/Arrogant)
	# Dismiss predecessor, seize the spotlight
	# ================================================
	option = {
		name = gr_option_forgotten
		
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_trait = ambitious
				has_trait = arrogant
			}
		}
		
		# Immediate prestige
		add_prestige = 2500
		
		# Monthly prestige +0.5 for 10 years
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gr_my_time_has_come
			years = 10
		}
		
		# Dynasty opinion -10 for 15 years
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gr_dishonored_remembrance
			years = 15
		}
		
		# Chronicler opinion -50 (permanent until death)
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gr_chronicler }
			var:gr_chronicler = {
				add_opinion = {
					target = root
					modifier = gr_rebuked_remembrancer
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	# ================================================
	# OPTION 4: "Glory to the Divine" (Zealous/Humble)
	# Honor the gods above mortal achievement
	# ================================================
	option = {
		name = gr_option_divine
		
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_trait = zealous
				has_trait = humble
			}
		}
		
		# Immediate piety
		add_piety = 1500
		
		# Monthly piety +1.0 for 10 years
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gr_glory_to_the_divine
			years = 10
		}
		
		# Clergy opinion +15 and same faith opinion +10 for 15 years
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gr_divine_remembrance
			years = 15
		}
		
		# Chronicler opinion -25 (permanent until death)
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gr_chronicler }
			var:gr_chronicler = {
				add_opinion = {
					target = root
					modifier = gr_rebuked_remembrancer_light
				}
			}
		}
		
		# Court priest opinion +50 for 15 years
		if = {
			limit = { exists = cp:councillor_court_chaplain }
			cp:councillor_court_chaplain = {
				add_opinion = {
					target = root
					modifier = gr_pious_remembrance_chaplain
					years = 15
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	# ================================================
	# OPTION 5: "The People's Remembrance" (Generous/Gregarious)
	# Honor ruler and common folk alike
	# ================================================
	option = {
		name = gr_option_honor_all
		
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_trait = generous
				has_trait = gregarious
			}
		}
		
		# Immediate prestige + piety
		add_prestige = 500
		add_piety = 500
		
		# Monthly prestige +0.25 and piety +0.5 for 10 years
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gr_peoples_remembrance
			years = 10
		}
		
		# General opinion +20 for 15 years
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = gr_remembered_with_honor
			years = 15
		}
	}
	
	# ================================================
